Use when documenting a specific screen. - **screen-spec-fleet**: Generate one spec document per screen in parallel from Figma frames, one worker per screen, merging SCREEN_MAP.md and routes.md from their partials; the orchestrator-workers variant of screen-spec. Use when the screen count is 6 or more, a Figma MCP is reachable, and foundations plus SCREEN_MAP exist. Do not use for a single screen (use screen-spec), when foundations are missing (use design-bootstrap first), or when frames are not yet enumerated (caller supplies number, slug, persona and node id per screen).
